## 80. Ceiling (i) Reopens Radiation — the History-Generated Magnetic-Analog Carries Radius-Independent Far-Field Angular-Momentum Flux Under the Declared Effective Reconstruction (2026-07-11)

> **WITHDRAWN by the §82 audit (2026-07-11).** The "radius-independent flux ⇒ radiation channel exists" verdict is CIRCULAR: this section analytically inserts $B_r=O(r^{-2})$ and $B_\phi=O(r^{-1})$, then integrates $r^3\,B_rB_\phi = r^0$ — radius-independent BY CONSTRUCTION. The "constant flux $\approx58$" only echoes the powers fed to the post-processor, and its magnitude swings $\sim426\to5$ with the regulator and $\sim118\to28$ with the derivative step: not a converged observable. Moreover the $\mathbf A_{\rm wake}=\sum_s\kappa q_s\mathbf v_s/r$ with $\mathbf B=\nabla\times\mathbf A_{\rm wake}$ is a **surrogate**, not the canonical law: the curl manufactures a $\partial_t(1/r)$ radiation-like term the central $W^{\mathrm{rec}}$ force does not contain, and it catastrophically cancels at the rail source-normal caustic ($D_s\to0$). §82 confirms the surrogate is non-convergent (slope swings $+0.06\to+2.5$, magnitude $\times89$) AND, decisively, re-measures the magnetic/antisymmetric channel from the CANONICAL $W^{\mathrm{rec}}$ force ($\mathbf E_{\mathrm{anti}}=\mathbf E_{\mathrm{full}}-\mathbf E_{\mathrm{static}}$): it is **BOUND** (soft-extrapolated, caustic-resolution-converged), the same "no $1/r$ tail" class as the electric channel. So this section's "a radiation channel exists" is WRONG: the surrogate's apparent radiation was an artifact; the canonical channel does not radiate. The positive control (a genuine outgoing helical $m{=}1$ field) is legitimate and is re-used in §82. See §82.

Executing the Section 79 magnetic-analog ceiling. New probe `magneticAnalogFarFieldFlux` (CLI `--magnetic-analog-flux`) consumes a 2048-sample periodic retained record of the prescribed V5 worldlines and reconstructs the effective vector transport potential

$$
\mathbf A_{\mathrm{wake}}(\mathbf X,t)
=
\sum_s
\frac{\kappa q_s\mathbf v_s(t_e)}{r_s}
\frac{D_s}{D_s^2+\epsilon^2},
\qquad
t-t_e=\frac{r_s}{c_f},
\qquad
D_s=c_f-\mathbf v_s(t_e)\cdot\hat{\mathbf r}_s.
$$

This is a **declared effective modeling choice** for the schematic vector-potential channel in the effective Lagrangian, not an independently derived exact field law. The post-processor takes the leading far-zone curl $\mathbf B_{\mathrm{wake}}=\nabla\times\mathbf A_{\mathrm{wake}}$: $B_r=O(r^{-2})$ and $B_\phi=O(r^{-1})$. The declared magnetic-type Maxwell stress is

$$
T^B_{ij}=B_iB_j-\frac12\delta_{ij}B^2,
\qquad
\Phi_{\mathrm{mag}}(r)
=
\left\langle\oint r\sin\theta\,B_rB_\phi\,dA\right\rangle.
$$

**Positive control.** The same integrator receives the analytic outgoing helical $m=1$ potential $A_\theta\propto\sin\theta\cos[\phi-\omega(t-r/c_f)]/r$. It returns the same flux magnitude $4.3780235371$ at every radius from $16$ through $256$, endpoint slope $0.000000$, radial spread $2.84\times10^{-15}$. The stress integrator therefore detects a known radiating antisymmetric source and distinguishes constant flux from the §78 bound falloff.

## 82. The Corrected Radiation / Self-Torque Instrument (Audit Rebuild of §§78–81): the ELECTRIC/Branch Channel Is BOUND (Measured, Reported as a Slope Interval), the MAGNETIC/Antisymmetric Channel Reconstructed From the CANONICAL $W^{\mathrm{rec}}$ Force Is Also BOUND (Measured — the $\mathbf A_{\mathrm{wake}}$ Curl Surrogate Was a Non-Convergent Artifact), the Honest Net Self-Torque Is +0.424 as a HELD-SEED Diagnostic, and the Conservation Identity Stays Open — the Rigid-Circular Braid Does Not Radiate, but S1/S2 Is Not Thereby Closed (2026-07-11)

An adversarial audit found the entire §78–§81 radiation arc rests on circular and unconverged measurements (see the WITHDRAWN banners on §§78–81). This section rebuilds the instrument under the audit's rules and reports a claim-leveled verdict. Probes `correctedRadiationInstrument` (CLI `--corrected-radiation`), `canonicalMagneticFarFieldFlux` (CLI `--canonical-magnetic`), and `honestNetSelfTorque` (CLI `--honest-self-torque`); central solver untouched; runner + fixtures only; owner suite green (full-pipeline control + completeness-of-roots + electric-interval + canonical-magnetic + β-onset tests added). Fail-closed; seed grade.

**The canonical force is central; there is no separate magnetic force.** The per-hit law (master-equation.md §"receiver-normal branch factor") is $\mathbf A=\kappa\sigma|q_iq_j|/r^2\,W^{\mathrm{rec}}\,\hat{\mathbf r}$ with $W^{\mathrm{rec}}=|D_T/D_s|$, $D_T=c_f-\hat{\mathbf r}\cdot\mathbf V_i$, $D_s=c_f-\hat{\mathbf r}\cdot\mathbf V_j(t_e)$, and the Lemma (Circular receiver-normal cancellation) gives $D_T=D_s\Rightarrow W^{\mathrm{rec}}=1$ on every nondegenerate rigid-circular root. At a STATIONARY measurement sphere $\mathbf V_i=0$, so $D_T=c_f$ and the one field the law predicts is $\mathbf E=\sum_s\kappa q_s(1/|D_s|)_{\mathrm{reg}}\hat{\mathbf r}_s/r_s^2$. The "magnetic/antisymmetric" channel is the **velocity-odd part** of this single field, $\mathbf E_{\mathrm{anti}}=\mathbf E_{\mathrm{full}}-\mathbf E_{\mathrm{static}}$ (dropping the $D_s$ branch factor). The §80 $\mathbf A_{\mathrm{wake}}=\sum_s\kappa q_s\mathbf v_s/r$ with $\mathbf B=\nabla\times\mathbf A_{\mathrm{wake}}$ is a **surrogate**: the curl manufactures a $\partial_t(1/r)$ term the force law does not contain.

**What was rebuilt.** (1) The field is reconstructed at every finite sphere DIRECTLY from the worldlines with **no imposed $1/R$ power**; the measured falloff decides bound vs radiative. (2) A **complete** causal-root certifier: it brackets zeros of both $g$ **and** $g'$, so a tangent/double root (a branch being born, where $g$ touches $0$ without crossing) is detected rather than silently dropped as "zero residual = no root"; it reports root-count stability under scan refinement and exposes inactive-root gaps (the source-normal Jacobian floor). Tangent-root and near-tangent completeness regressions are added, and `honestNetSelfTorque` now calls this certifier instead of a private sign-change scan. (3) The stress integrand carries the full $R\sin\theta$ lever for both channels. (4) Controls are two-tier: `quadratureControls` (analytic fields fed straight to the quadrature — validate the quadrature + curl operator ONLY; radiator slope $0.000$, bound slope $-1.000$, curl-op rel. err $\sim4\times10^{-5}$) are DEMOTED, and a **full-pipeline control** is added and REQUIRED — a manufactured single point-charge circular history whose exact canonical Coulomb-from-causal-delay field is reconstructed through the SAME certified-root + regularization + quadrature pipeline (field rel. err $\sim4\times10^{-6}$; the bound $1/r^2$ source correctly returns a vanishing far flux, slope $\approx-2.0$). The instrument is trustworthy on the actual pipeline before it touches braid data.

**Result 1 — the ELECTRIC/branch channel is BOUND (measured), reported as a slope INTERVAL.** Across the regulator sweep and the fit-window sweep the robust aggregate slopes (endpoint + LSQ) lie in $[\approx-3.4,\approx-1.9]$ and the outer flux is $\sim10^{-3}$ of the $+0.076$ residual at every regulator — a **stable vanishing flux**. "Bound" is defined as this stable vanishing flux compared against the instrument's own analytic bound control ($-1$), not as a slope$<-1.5$ threshold; the §78 "converged $-2.0$" is corrected to a reported interval (intermediate-$r$ window sub-slopes carry the cancellation-prone $\langle E_\phi E_r\rangle$ quadrature noise and are reported, not gated on). The §78 *direction* (a force law with no acceleration term produces a bound $\sim1/r^2$ field) survives; its single converged number does not. **Claim level: measured (stable vanishing flux; slope interval).**

**Result 2 — the MAGNETIC/antisymmetric channel, reconstructed from the CANONICAL $W^{\mathrm{rec}}$ force, is also BOUND (measured).** $\mathbf E_{\mathrm{anti}}=\mathbf E_{\mathrm{full}}-\mathbf E_{\mathrm{static}}$ is a $1/r^2$ field (no acceleration term), so its angular-momentum flux $\Phi_{\mathrm{anti}}(r)$ falls with $r$: endpoint slopes $\approx-2.0,-2.1,-2.4,-2.5$ across $\mathrm{soft}\in\{0.08,0.04,0.02,0.01\}$ (slope range $\approx0.5$), outer flux $\sim2\times10^{-3}$ of the residual, and — the audit's explicit demand before attributing non-convergence to an irreducible caustic — a **caustic-resolution scaling** at the tightest regulator (double the angular/time grid) leaves the outer flux finite and a vanishing fraction of the residual (it shifts by tens of percent, as a caustic-affected integral does, but does NOT grow without bound): the near-$D_s{=}0$ (equatorial rail) contribution is integrable, not an irreducible caustic. So the hypothesis holds: the rigid-circular braid is **bound on the magnetic channel too**, the same "no $1/r$ tail" class as the electric channel. By contrast the $\mathbf A_{\mathrm{wake}}$ curl **surrogate** is non-convergent (slope swings $\sim2.5$, magnitude swings $\times\!\sim7$ across the regulator/step sweep) — that non-convergence is a **curl artifact** (the $\partial_t/r$ term is not in the force law) and the §80 "constant flux $\approx58$" was radius-independent BY CONSTRUCTION ($R^3\cdot R^{-2}\cdot R^{-1}=R^0$). Canonical regularization prescription (declared): $W^{\mathrm{rec}}\to D_s/(D_s^2+\mathrm{soft}^2)$, the quadratic stress evaluated on the regularized field, the verdict read from the $\mathrm{soft}\to0$ extrapolation. **Claim level: measured bound at seed grade under the declared electric-type stress and this regularization prescription.**

**Result 3 — the honest net self-torque is $+0.424$, a HELD-SEED diagnostic; the self-hit is the coincidence only.** The whole-braid net secular z-torque, summed over all six sites from the delayed partner wake with the complete certifier (max residual $\sim10^{-15}$, root count stable, no unstable hits) and period-averaged (transient-free), is $+0.424$ — $N_t$-converged and soft-robust, concentrated on the middle ($M=+0.4229$; $I,O\approx0$). The self-hit is the **analytic coincidence only**: at $\beta_M=1$ the self chart obeys $\sin\xi=\xi/\beta$, which has no positive-delay root for $\beta\le1$; a branch is born only for $\beta=1+\mu$ at $\xi_0\approx\sqrt{6\mu}$ — the certifier confirms this onset (measured $\xi_0=0.344$ at $\mu=0.02$ vs $\sqrt{0.12}=0.346$; $0.538$ at $\mu=0.05$ vs $0.548$), so the numerical self channel is **not** independently reconstructed at the caustic — only the coincidence stratum, labeled `analyticNoPositiveDelaySelfHitAtExactBetaOne`. The §81 "$+0.142$" is exactly $+0.424-0.667\times0.4229$: the held-seed partner torque minus the §66 *maximum* brake applied algebraically. The $+0.424$ is the **internal partner torque on the held rigid seed**; the **external holding torque a prescribed rigid V5 worldline requires** is $-0.424$. Root COMPLETENESS (not just residual) is now tested and clean; the labels are held-seed diagnostics, not "root-certified free-particle" quantities. **Claim level: the held-seed torque is a robust measurement; the free-particle self-torque is unmeasured (needs a native force-free release).**

**Result 4 — the conservation identity stays open, for a cleaner reason.** The correct period-averaged balance is $\langle\tau_{\rm mech}\rangle+\langle\Phi_{\rm out}\rangle=0$ (a MINUS sign). It cannot be closed in a common normalization at this grade: the field is **bound on both channels** (Results 1–2), so there is no far-field flux to balance the $+0.424$ — which is exactly what a HELD-SEED (prescribed-worldline) torque should look like: an external holding torque, not a radiated one. Closing the identity for a *free particle* requires a native force-free release (so the worldline, not a prescription, supplies $\tau_{\rm mech}$) and an independently established field stress-energy normalization (still declared here). **Claim level: the free-particle conservation balance is unmeasured; the blocker is named and is no longer a non-convergent channel.**

**Verdict (claim-leveled).** Does the braid's actual field fall as $1/r^2$ (bound) or carry a $1/r$ radiation tail? — **Both channels are bound.** The electric/branch channel is bound (measured, stable vanishing flux, slope interval); the magnetic/antisymmetric channel reconstructed from the canonical $W^{\mathrm{rec}}$ force is **also bound** (measured, soft-extrapolated, caustic-resolution-converged). **The rigid-circular braid does not radiate to the far field at seed grade** — the $\mathbf A_{\mathrm{wake}}$ curl surrogate's apparent radiation was a non-convergent artifact of a term the force law does not contain. The transient-rejected net self-torque is $+0.424$ as a **held-seed** diagnostic; no free-particle secular self-torque is established. The conservation identity does not close for a free particle (undetermined field stress-energy normalization; needs a native release). **Both the §78/§79 "bound/reactive/S1S2 closes" and the §80/§81 "radiates/reopens" verdicts remain WITHDRAWN**: the field is bound, but that does not by itself close S1/S2 (the near-field $+0.076$ is a held-seed property, and the free-particle release is what decides the shape/size attractor). What §82 *does* settle at seed grade: the far-field radiation question — the rigid-circular braid is bound on both channels. Fail-closed: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`.

Reproduce: `node scripts/braid-ideal/spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.mjs --corrected-radiation --pretty`, `--canonical-magnetic --pretty`, and `--honest-self-torque --pretty`.

Next closure goal: to lift the magnetic-analog channel from unmeasured to measured, replace the caustic-dominated finite-difference curl of $A_{\rm wake}$ with a regulator-independent reconstruction that handles the source-normal caustic at $\beta_M=1$ (an analytic curl on a caustic-adapted chart, or a native retained-history field that does not pass through the $D_s\to0$ weight), and establish the field stress-energy normalization so $\mathbf C_{ij}^{(\eta)}$, $\mathbf A_{\rm wake}$, and $\Pi_{q,J}^{[ij]}$ can be compared in a common normalization and the $\langle\tau_{\rm mech}\rangle+\langle\Phi_{\rm out}\rangle=0$ identity tested; the decisive lift is a native force-free release (not a prescribed rigid worldline) whose secular self-torque and open-boundary flux can be measured together.

**Instrument hardening (D1–D4, 2026-07-11).** `correctedRadiationInstrument` gained an end-to-end **radiative positive control**: the same manufactured single-charge history, reconstructed through the certified-root + regularization + quadrature pipeline, PLUS an injected analytic transverse $1/r$ tail; the pipeline must FLAG it (flux slope $\approx0$, radius-independent) — so `controlsPass` now validates the pipeline on both a bound source (vanishing flux) and a radiating source, not compositionally. Reproduced slopes: bound control $-2.02$, radiative control $+0.004$. The electric/branch and magnetic/antisymmetric channels are annotated as **nested diagnostics of one central field** ($\mathbf E_{\mathrm{anti}}=\mathbf E_{\mathrm{full}}-\mathbf E_{\mathrm{static}}\subset\mathbf E_{\mathrm{full}}$); the loose `resolutionConverges` flag is renamed `causticContributionIntegrable`; and the withdrawn §78/§81 probe-tests are labeled reproducibility-only inline.

**Result 2 — the bounded non-rigid torque-null search finds NO free bound braid (verdict).** Before concluding "no free stable braid at this scale without an environment," a bounded neighborhood of V5 was searched for a configuration that is simultaneously **torque-free** (net secular z-torque $\to0$) and **radially force-free** (a single $\kappa^\ast$ makes the delayed wake supply every layer's centripetal need at small residual — the residual `globalRelResidual` includes any unmatched tangential wake force, so a large rail pump shows up directly as residual). - **Speed axis.** The net secular z-torque is **sign-definite positive across the whole window** — it never crosses zero: $+0.043$ at $\beta{=}0.4$, $+0.424$ at the pin $\beta{=}1$, $+0.523$ at $\beta{=}1.2$ (monotone increasing). The force-free residual moves the OPPOSITE way: $0.665$ at $\beta{=}0.4$, $0.427$ at the pin, $0.388$ at $\beta{=}1.2$. So lowering $\beta$ reduces the pump toward zero (reached only in the $\beta\to0$ static, no-orbit limit) while WORSENING radial support; raising $\beta$ improves support while worsening the pump.
- **Tilt axis.** Net z-torque is positive for every tilt in $\pm16^\circ$ (min $+0.254$ at $-16^\circ$); the residual is smallest ($0.36$) near a moderate negative tilt but still an order above a genuinely force-free orbit.
- **Verdict.** No configuration in the bounded family is both torque-free and radially supported; the smallest force-free residual anywhere is $0.36\gg0.05$. The rail pump (whose $\beta_M=1$ pin IS radial-support closure, §58) and torque-freedom are the **same single degree of freedom pulling in opposite directions**: the bare rigid braid cannot satisfy both. Claim level: **held-family force-free-landscape map, seed grade, bounded two-knob search; not a native release; no release authorized.**

**Consequence for $A_0$ and the mass map.** The real $A_0$ (a bounded self-consistent force-free periodic orbit — the S1/S2 shape/size attractor) is **not** realized by the bare braid at this scale: the size anchor $R_M^{\mathrm{eq}}=3.4937\,\kappa\varepsilon^2/c_f^2$, $\kappa_{\mathrm{eq}}=0.28623$ stands as the size-chart intake (mass-map priorities item 6), but $E_{\mathrm{internal}}(A_0)$ is **not** extractable — its extraction remains gated on the structured-environment / non-rigid-axis realization that both closes the axis sector (§61 flutter) and supplies the angular-momentum sink for the un-nullable rail pump. ## 84. The Per-Layer-Independent-$\omega$ Torque-Null Landscape: Differential Rotation and Counter-Rotation CAN Null the Pump, but Only by Breaking Closure and Radial Support — the Representable Locus Stays Sign-Definite, So No Bare Braid (Rigid OR Differential) Is a Free Particle (2026-07-11)

Executing the §83 independent lever: the last untested crack. §83 closed the RIGID co-rotating family (single $\omega$, varied by $c_{\mathrm{Trans}}$ and $\alpha_M$) — the net secular z-torque is sign-definite positive and no bare rigid orbit is both torque-free and radially supported. §83 named one escape it did not test: PER-LAYER-INDEPENDENT frequencies $(\omega_I,\omega_M,\omega_O)$ — differential rotation and counter-rotation. This section maps that family and adds the closure gate that decides whether a differential torque-null is a genuine braid. Runner + fixture only (central solver `AbsoluteHistoryRootRuntime.mjs` untouched): `braidPerOmegaEvaluate`, `braidClosureRigidity`, and `perLayerOmegaTorqueNullSearch` added to [spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.mjs](../../../../scripts/braid-ideal/spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.mjs), CLI `--torque-null-perlayer`; owner suite green. Held-family seed grade; no native release authorized.

**The instrument.** `braidPerOmegaEvaluate` reuses the certified-root partner-wake block of `braidNetZTorque` (§83), generalized to a per-layer angular rate $\omega_L=m_L\,\omega_0$ with $m_L$ an independent multiplier on the base cadence, $m_L<0$ = counter-rotation. It cycle-averages over the COMPOSITE period (the smallest integer number $K$ of base periods on which every layer returns to phase; rational $m_L$) and reports BOTH the net secular z-torque and the single-$\kappa^\ast$ force-free residual, fitted on the same cycle-averaged samples. The middle stays on the rail ($m_M=1$, the radial-support/size anchor) in the primary grid. Regression: the iso-frequency point $m_I=m_M=m_O=1$ reproduces §83/jh9 exactly — net $+0.4240$, middle $+0.4229$, $I/O\approx0$, force-free residual $0.4265$ (§83 pin $0.427$), $K=1$.

**The closure gate (representability).** Independent per-layer $\omega$ breaks the iso-frequency condition that makes the nested-tilted binary a single coherent closed braid (§9 $\Delta E=\hbar\omega$ partition identity, §15 iso-frequency rail closure, §18 harmonic matching). A genuine closed braid is RIGID in a common co-rotating frame — there is one frame rotation rate in which the whole configuration is static (the §24 screw-rigidity witness, generalized). `braidClosureRigidity` takes the middle-rail rate $\Omega=m_M\,\omega_0$ as the candidate common frame and measures how far each layer drifts in it over the composite period, normalized to the site radius. Iso-frequency is static there (rigidity residual $=4.4\times10^{-16}$, representable); any differential or counter-rotating layer sweeps a full circle in the frame (rigidity residual $\sim0.85$–$0.89$, order one — a decomposition of independently spinning layers, NOT a representable closed braid).

**Result — differential/counter-rotation CAN null the pump, but only off the representable, radially-supported locus.** The grid over $(m_I,m_O)\in\{-1,-\tfrac12,\tfrac12,1,\tfrac32,2\}$ at $m_M=1$ (with an iso-frequency common-multiplier sub-scan) gives three findings that compose into the verdict.

- **The representable locus is sign-definite positive.** The iso-frequency common-multiplier scan reproduces §83's rigid speed family exactly: net $+0.0428$ at $m{=}0.4$, $+0.424$ at the pin $m{=}1$, $+0.5227$ at $m{=}1.2$ (§83 reported $+0.043\to+0.523$), residual $0.665\to0.388$. Every representable row has net $>0$.
- **Differential rotation and counter-rotation drive the net z-torque through zero — a real crossing.** The grid net ranges $-0.077$ to $+0.585$; counter-rotating the INNER layer ($m_I<0$) opposes the middle's angular momentum and nulls, then reverses, the pump. Seventeen torque-null points (grid cells plus interpolated $m_O$ crossings) were located.
- **Every one of them fails BOTH conditions the torque-null needs.** (i) Closure: each torque-null point has a co-rotating-frame rigidity residual of order one ($0.85$–$0.89$) — it is not a single coherent braid but independently spinning layers, so it does not count as a representable free particle. (ii) Radial support: off the iso-frequency locus the wake power moves into oscillating harmonics that a circular orbit cannot absorb (the §18 harmonic-matching principle; §18/§19 league table $0.85$–$1.0$ vs the $0.646$ iso floor), so the single-$\kappa^\ast$ force-free residual at every torque-null point is $\approx0.98$–$0.99$. The smallest force-free residual anywhere in the entire per-layer family is $0.3882$ (at the iso pin $m{=}1.2$), still an order above a genuinely force-free orbit.

**Verdict.** `per_layer_omega_search_finds_NO_representable_radially_supported_torque_free_configuration`. The two degrees of freedom a differential torque-null would need — representable closure and radial support — are exactly the two that the differential/counter-rotation freedom destroys. The torque-null and the "closed, radially-supported braid" loci are disjoint by construction: the only way to null the sign-definite $+0.424$ rail pump with per-layer frequencies is to counter-spin a layer, which simultaneously (a) unbraids the object (rigidity residual $\to$ order one) and (b) collapses radial support (residual $\to\approx0.99$). This closes the last §83 crack. Combined with the rigid family, **no bare braid — rigid OR differential — is simultaneously torque-free, radially supported, and a closed braid**: "no bare braid is a free particle" is now airtight across the whole two-family (rigid + per-layer-$\omega$) landscape. The un-nullable rail pump requires an external angular-momentum sink (a structured sea / environment), consistent with the §70–75 local-sink no-go and the mass-map $A_0$ intake gate (item 6). Caveat (declared): this is a RIGID-per-layer family (each layer in uniform circular motion at its own constant $\omega_L$; the middle pinned on the rail); a per-layer live-$\omega(t)$ modulation (harmonically-forced response orbit, §18) is the residual untested freedom, but §18 already scores that class $\gtrsim0.65$ on the radial ledger. Claim level: **held-family per-layer-$\omega$ force-free-landscape map, seed grade; not a native release; no release authorized.** Fail-closed: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`.

**Consequence for the convergence claim.** With the net z-torque sign-definite across the entire representable independent-$\omega$ family, the corpus statement clears its last hedge: the bare braid is not a free particle, and the self-consistent braid+sea complex is the load-bearing object for $A_0$. The §83 caveat ("rigid family only; per-layer-$\omega$ search future work") is discharged — the braid+sea-complex convergence can be promoted to corpus at effective grade, with the size-only $A_0$ intake ($R_M^{\mathrm{eq}}=3.4937\,\kappa\varepsilon^2/c_f^2$, $\kappa_{\mathrm{eq}}=0.28623$) standing and $E_{\mathrm{internal}}(A_0)$ still gated on the structured-environment realization.

Reproduce: `node scripts/braid-ideal/spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.mjs --torque-null-perlayer --pretty`.

Next closure goal: promote the braid+sea-complex convergence to `content/markdown/aaa` at effective grade (the bare braid is not a free particle, rigid + differential; the sink is the environment), restating the size-only $A_0$ intake and the open $E_{\mathrm{internal}}$ gate in reader-facing prose; then build the one structured-sea realization that both absorbs the sign-definite $+0.424$ rail-pump z-torque and closes the §61 axis flutter, and re-run the native force-free release inside it.

## 85. The First Global-Drain Dynamical-Sea Instrument (Coarse Pilot): a Structured Dynamical Sea Coupled to Measure the Axial-$L$ Export AND the §61 Flutter in One Object — the Conservative Co-Orbital Cage Exports ≈0 Secular $L_z$ (Does Not Drain the Pump) but Acquires Its OWN Secular z-Torque, the Saturable Channel Is Capped and Non-Vanishing in the Instantaneous Limit, and the Flutter Needs ≈400× the Measured Sea Damping — No Self-Consistent Drain-Plus-Quieting Object at Seed Grade; the Native Release Stays Gated (2026-07-11)

Executing the §84 next closure goal (the structured-sea realization that must simultaneously absorb the sign-definite $+0.424$ rail-pump z-torque and close the §61 axis flutter). This is the **first** native diagnostic that measures the drain as a **global axial-angular-momentum ($L_z$) transfer** — not the local equatorial-rail tangential force every prior probe used (§§71–75) — and engages the flutter in the **same** object, with a self-consistency / representability gate. Coarse pilot, build + run only: runner + fixture only (`globalDrainDynamicalSea` added to [spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.mjs](../../../../scripts/braid-ideal/spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.mjs), CLI `--global-drain-sea`; central solver `AbsoluteHistoryRootRuntime.mjs` untouched; reuses the §74 co-orbital causal-root kernel, the §75 saturable-drain, the §61/§63 axis pencil, and the §67 sea damping estimate). Owner suite green (chunked by name-pattern). The expensive native force-free release inside the sea is **not** run here — it is gated on the adversarial global-drain feasibility verdict. Fail-closed; no release authorized.

**The instrument.** For a structured dynamical sea (the §55 octahedral cage co-orbiting about the spin axis at $\Omega=\mathrm{frac}\cdot\omega$, plus the §75 saturable reorienting-dipole response) coupled through the near-field channel, it books three readouts on the certified-root partner-wake block:

- **(1) $L$-export.** The cycle-averaged z-torque the braid exerts ON the sea endpoints (`Lexport`, the sea's $L_z$ gain rate) and, reciprocally, the sea exerts ON the braid (`braidReaction`, the braking of the $+z$ pump). Success is `Lexport` $\approx+$pump AND `braidReaction` $\approx-$pump, so the braid's net secular z-torque (`braidNetZTorque.net` $=+0.424$, the target reused verbatim from §82/§84) is drained to zero. A third channel, `seaOwnPump`, books the net secular z-torque AMONG the sea endpoints — the relocation witness.
- **(2) Flutter growth.** The bare whirl growth (`gyroscopicTiltAnalysisFull`, §61/§63) versus the growth with the sea's off-equatorial damping wired into the velocity block (the `extraDamping` requirement-mapping knob), swept to locate the damping magnitude that turns the whirl restoring, then compared to the sea's MEASURED off-equatorial authority (`seaTiltDampingEstimate`, §67).
- **(3) Self-consistency / representability gate.** Flags an EXTERNAL HOLD — a braking torque with no reciprocal sea $L$-gain (the §82 $-0.424$ held-partner analogue), or a drain that survives the §75 instantaneous-limit guard (a genuine dissipative drain must vanish as $\gamma\to\infty$) — and the sea acquiring its OWN un-nulled secular z-torque (the drain relocating the obstruction, the §10/§14 cross-hit relay at the sea level).

Regression (verified): with `withSea:false` the cage is empty, `Lexport = braidReaction = seaOwnPump = 0` at every co-orbit rate, the dressed flutter sweep is the bare pencil to machine precision, and `braidNetZTorque` reproduces the pump $+0.424$ (middle-carried $+0.4229$, $I/O\approx0$) — the bare braid reproduces the sign-definite pump and the flutter growth exactly.

**Result 1 — the conservative co-orbital cage exports ≈0 secular $L_z$ (no drain) but acquires its OWN secular z-torque.** Across co-orbit rates $\mathrm{frac}\in\{0,\tfrac12,1\}$ the braid$\to$sea $L$-export and the sea$\to$braid reaction are both $\approx0$ (below $10^{-4}$; `maxLexportFracOfPump` $=0$): a symmetric octahedral shell cannot receive a net cycle-averaged z-torque from the braid, and returns none — the §74 "zero secular transfer" re-measured as an $L_z$ book, so the conservative co-orbital cage does **not** drain the $+0.424$ pump. But the co-orbiting sea's INTERNAL net z-torque is large and non-zero at the intermediate rate: `seaOwnPump` $=0$ at $\mathrm{frac}=0$ (static shell, symmetric) and at $\mathrm{frac}=1$ (rigid co-rotation, stationary in the co-rotating frame), and $\approx-0.65$ at $\mathrm{frac}=\tfrac12$ (154% of the pump; the §55 $-0.641$ polar-pull reappearing as a differential-co-orbit z-torque, the causal delays breaking the shell's symmetry exactly as §18's harmonic mismatch does). The one rate at which the sea carries secular angular momentum is a rate at which the sea pumps ITSELF — the obstruction relocated inward, not dissipated.

**Result 2 — the saturable reorienting channel is capped and non-vanishing in the instantaneous limit; the flutter needs ≈400× the measured sea authority.** The §75 saturable dipole (reused at the §72 anchored-oblique base tilt where the transport gate is open) gives a nonlinear multiple $\approx1.10$, native drain $\approx0.015$ against a transported target $\approx0.083$ — short by $\approx5\times$ (`clearsShortfall` false), and its instantaneous-limit guard drain is $\approx0.024$, NOT vanishing, so the small drain it does show does not cleanly separate from an imposed hold (external-hold flag fires). The flutter is un-quieted: the bare whirl grows at $+0.199$ (§61/§68 reproduced), turning restoring only at a velocity-block damping $\approx1.6$ (of order the growth rate), while the sea's measured off-equatorial authority at the tilted braid is $\approx0.004$ (§67 inner-starvation) — a damping shortfall of $\approx390\times$.

**Verdict.** `drain_relocates_pump_into_the_sea_which_acquires_its_own_secular_z_torque_no_dissipation_hands_back_to_codex_corroborates_global_no_go`. At coarse seed grade no self-consistent dynamical medium both drains the pump and quiets the flutter: the conservative co-orbital channel transfers no secular $L_z$ to the sea (and the only secular $L_z$ the sea carries is its own relocated pump), the saturable dissipative channel is capped $\approx5\times$ short and does not vanish instantaneously, and the flutter requires damping two-plus orders above the sea's measured authority. The three obstructions the §84 realization had to clear jointly — the global $L$-drain, a settled axis anchor, and $\sigma\to0$ shape support — are not cleared by this dynamical sea; the pilot corroborates a possible **global** no-go (the drain merely relocating the obstruction, consistent with the §82 non-radiation result: a bound field with a real net self-torque needs a genuine external $L$-sink, and a conservative co-orbiting shell is not one). The expensive native force-free release inside the sea is therefore not warranted on this pilot and stays gated; the global-drain feasibility question — whether ANY representable, self-consistent dynamical medium can supply a secular dissipative $L_z$-drain of a bound braid's sign-definite pump without acquiring its own un-nulled pump — is handed to the adversary. Claim level: **coarse pilot, native seed grade, prescribed-worldline reuse of certified sub-blocks; not a native release; no release authorized.** Fail-closed: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`.

Reproduce: `node scripts/braid-ideal/spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.mjs --global-drain-sea --pretty` (bare regression: add `--no-sea`).

Next closure goal: on an adversarial CLEAR of the global-drain route (a representable, self-consistent dynamical medium that carries a secular dissipative $L_z$-drain without acquiring its own pump), lift this pilot to a settled-steady-state grade — a co-orbiting-plus-reorienting sea integrated to its own dynamical steady state, with the $L$-export measured as the sea's actual $L_z$ accumulation rate and the flutter read on the same object — and only then authorize the gated native force-free release of the braid+sea complex as a free particle; on an adversarial BAR (the drain is provably relocation-only or hold-only, confronting the §82 non-radiation and the pump-relocation regress), record the global no-go and return $A_0$'s $E_{\mathrm{internal}}$ to the far-field radiation-with-replenishment route (§76) as the sole surviving sink.

## 86. The Spin-Interleaving Flutter Sweep: Is the Axis-Sector Gyroscopic-Circulatory Flutter Spin-1/2 Precession Read in the Wrong Inter-Layer Interleaving? — No Admissible Interleaving Drives the Leading Eigenvalue Marginal; the Baseline Is the LEAST-Unstable Pattern and Still Grows ($+0.199$); Every Opposite-Sense (Alternating) Branch Reopens Closure and Radial/Tangential Support; the Axis Sector Stays Closed at Seed Grade (2026-07-11)

Attacking the axis-sector obstruction independent of the §85 drain front. The live axis-sector killer is the gyroscopic-circulatory flutter at the self-equilibrated V5 fixed point: the honest pencil $P(\lambda)=\lambda^2 M+\lambda(G-D)+\Gamma-K$ gives a growing internal whirl (leading deflated $\mathrm{Re}\,\lambda=+0.199>0$, whirl frequency $2.41$), even though the radial basin, the tangential ledger, and the static tilt block are all restoring at V5. This section tests one hypothesis: that the flutter is not an instability at all but **spin-1/2 precession read in the wrong inter-layer interleaving**. A genuine precession is marginal ($\mathrm{Re}\,\lambda=0$, $\mathrm{Im}\,\lambda\neq0$); a flutter grows. The spindle is a uniaxial nested-tilted-binary knot (inner/middle/outer), and the corpus has not committed to how the three binaries' precessions interleave — two topologically distinct patterns (co-cyclic corotating helix vs alternating adjacent-$\pi$/opposite-sense) differ in $4\pi$-return. Runner + fixture only (central solver `AbsoluteHistoryRootRuntime.mjs` untouched): `gyroscopicTiltAnalysisFull` extended with regression-safe interleaving parameters (`dTheta`, `sense`), plus `interleavedFlutterPoint`, `interleavingFlutterSweep`, and `fourPiReturnProbe` added to [spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.mjs](../../../../scripts/braid-ideal/spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.mjs), CLI `--interleaving-sweep`; owner suite green (chunked). Seed-grade linear stability; not a native release; no release authorized.

**The parameterization.** Each layer $L$ gets a precession phase $\varphi_L$ about the common axis (an azimuthal offset `dTheta[L]` added to the baseline orbital phase $\theta_L$) and a sense $s_L\in\{+,-\}$ (`sense[L]`, the sign of the layer's rate and spin angular momentum $J_L=2\rho_L^2\,\omega\,s_L$). Because $|s_L|=1$ every layer keeps the same $|\omega|$, so the single-period $2\pi/\omega$ cycle average stays exact under any interleaving. Defaults $\varphi_L=0$, $s_L=+1$ reproduce the current (co-cyclic, common-rate) interleaving. The full pencil $M,G,\Gamma,K$ and the delay-memory rate block $D$ are re-measured on the interleaved worldlines by the same cycle-averaged $\kappa^\ast$-scaled exact-causal-root evaluator; the leading DEFLATED root (global-tilt double zero deflated first) is read out — $\mathrm{Re}\,\lambda$ the growth, $\mathrm{Im}\,\lambda$ the whirl frequency. **Regression (verified bit-exact):** the default interleaving reproduces $\mathrm{Re}\,\lambda=+0.198857$, $\mathrm{Im}\,\lambda=2.41246$ — the known $+0.199$-class flutter.

**Gate discipline.** A marginal reading counts only if the three already-closed sectors stay closed at that interleaving: the static tilt block (relative/quotient spectrum of the symmetric $x$-tilt Jacobian $A$ restoring, the `tiltStiffness` reduction read off the interleaved pencil), radial/tangential support (single-$\kappa^\ast$ force-free residual not materially worse than the co-cyclic iso floor $0.4265$, via `braidPerOmegaEvaluate`), and closure (a representable coherent braid, rigid in a common co-rotating frame, via `braidClosureRigidity`). Support and closure depend only on the sense pattern, not the precession phase (azimuthal covariance of the cycle-averaged wake); the phase enters the flutter through the RELATIVE layer geometry.

**Result 1 — the co-cyclic branch: relative rephasing only worsens the flutter, never approaches the imaginary axis.** Sweeping the relative precession phases $(\varphi_M,\varphi_O)$ over $[0,2\pi)$ with $s=(+,+,+)$, every point has $\mathrm{Re}\,\lambda>0$ with a nonzero whirl: the grid minimum is $\approx+0.18$ and the maximum $\approx+2.0$. A local coordinate descent minimizing $|\mathrm{Re}\,\lambda|$ over the gate-closed region settles back at the baseline ($\varphi\approx0$), $|\mathrm{Re}\,\lambda|\approx0.198$ — **the current interleaving is the LEAST-unstable co-cyclic pattern, and it still grows.** A global azimuthal shift (uniform $\varphi_L$) leaves the flutter invariant to $10^{-3}$ (the covariance validation row: a global rotation is a symmetry; relative rephasing is not). The co-cyclic$\to$alternating continuous morph (phase blended from the corotating helix $\varphi=(0,\tfrac{2\pi}{3},\tfrac{4\pi}{3})$ to the adjacent-$\pi$ $\varphi=(0,\pi,0)$) stays growing end to end ($\mathrm{Re}\,\lambda$ from $\approx1.0$ down to $\approx0.54$, never near $0$), and relative rephasing away from baseline can itself REOPEN the static tilt block — the co-cyclic gate is not free.

**Result 2 — the opposite-sense (alternating) branch changes the spectrum but reopens closure and support: it does not count.** Every counter-rotating sense pattern (the other reading of "alternating") is a non-representable braid (closure rigidity residual $0.42$–$0.86$, `representable=false`) with a force-free residual $0.77$–$0.99$ far above the $0.43$ iso floor — it reopens both the closure and the radial/tangential-support sectors, exactly the §84 differential-$\omega$ no-go re-encountered. Counter-rotating the middle collapses the whirl toward a real instability ($\mathrm{Im}\,\lambda\to0$) rather than a marginal precession, and several sense patterns give purely real growth. No opposite-sense point is admissible.

**Result 3 — the $4\pi$ check is not reached.** No admissible interleaving is marginal, so there is no conservative precession mode whose $2\pi$-vs-$4\pi$ return could be tested; the spin-1/2 secondary check is moot. For completeness the informative winding of the (growing) leading mode around a full $2\pi$ precession loop is INTEGER (one turn, no half-integer double-cover), but this is a candidate diagnostic only and carries no signature on a non-marginal mode.

**Verdict.** `flutter_is_a_genuine_instability_independent_of_interleaving_axis_sector_stays_closed`. Across the full co-cyclic interleaving landscape (relative precession phases over $[0,2\pi)$ and the co-cyclic$\to$alternating morph), every admissible pattern keeps the leading deflated pencil eigenvalue at $\mathrm{Re}\,\lambda>0$, with the admissible minimum $|\mathrm{Re}\,\lambda|\approx0.18$ bounded well away from zero — there is no $|\mathrm{Re}\,\lambda|<\text{tol}$ window and no zero crossing, so the "knife-edge marginal" concern for a non-conservative gyroscopic-circulatory pencil does not arise (the minimum is a bounded-away tangency, not a true crossing). The opposite-sense branches that could change the spectrum all reopen the closure and support sectors. **The axis-sector gyroscopic-circulatory flutter is a genuine instability of the bare braid, independent of how the layer precessions interleave; it is not spin-1/2 precession read in the wrong pattern.** The axis sector stays closed at seed grade and needs non-rigid axis dynamics or a structured environment, as currently stated — corroborating, from the flutter side, the §85 finding that the bare braid needs an external sink. Caveat (declared): rigid-per-layer interleaving family (per-layer precession phase and discrete $\pm$ sense; each layer in uniform circular motion at $|\omega|=\omega$); a per-layer live-$\omega(t)$ modulation (the §18 residual freedom, scoring $\gtrsim0.65$) is untested here, and the marginal-window robustness is read on a coarse grid plus local refine, not a certified continuation. Claim level: **seed-grade gyroscopic-pencil interleaving-landscape linear-stability map; not a native release; no release authorized.** Fail-closed: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`.

Reproduce: `node scripts/braid-ideal/spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.mjs --interleaving-sweep --pretty` (knobs `--grid-n`, `--chi-n`, `--nt`).

Next closure goal: with both fronts returning a bare-braid no-go — §85 (no self-consistent dynamical sea drains the pump and quiets the flutter) and §86 (the flutter is a genuine interleaving-independent instability, not mis-read spin) — the axis sector's surviving live freedom is per-layer live-$\omega(t)$ modulation (a harmonically-forced response orbit, §18) inside the structured-environment object; build that pencil and read the flutter on it, keeping the spin-1/2 reframe available only if that modulation opens an admissible marginal window.

## 87. The Same-Record Wake Angular-Momentum Ward Completion and the Balanced-Cell Transport Decider: the jh11 $-0.65$ Sits on a SUPERLUMINAL Co-Orbiting Caustic and Its Wake Completion Is Regulator-Non-Convergent (Route a), and the Minimal Balanced Pro/Anti Cell Is INTRINSICALLY PUMPED — the Reactive Cross-Hit Relay Absorbs Only $\approx2\%$ of the Sign-Definite Pump, So No Admissible Retained Sea Carries the Drain as a Transport Current (BARRED, Held to Proof) (2026-07-11)

Executing the deciding memo [retained-sea-angular-momentum-ward-identity-and-transport-kernel.md](retained-sea-angular-momentum-ward-identity-and-transport-kernel.md): supply the same-record wake angular-momentum density $\ell_z^{\mathrm{wake}}$ and current $J^i_{L_z,\mathrm{wake}}$ that a mechanical-only ledger lacks, and use them to decide whether a balanced sea cell can carry the braid's axial pump as a genuine transport current. The delayed pair law is non-reciprocal ($\mathbf F_{a\leftarrow b}(T)\neq-\mathbf F_{b\leftarrow a}(T)$), so the directed pair torques leave a Ward defect $q_{AB,z}$ and a mechanical/assembly-only $L_z$ ledger does not close; the jh11 $-0.65$ sea torque is $q_{AB,z}$ plus unresolved wake, an incomplete ledger, not demonstrated relocation or transport. Reusing the §82 canonical antisymmetric reconstruction ($\mathbf E_{\mathrm{anti}}=\mathbf E_{\mathrm{full}}-\mathbf E_{\mathrm{static}}$, the velocity-odd part of the one branch field) — not new field machinery. Runner + fixtures only: `sameRecordWakeAngularMomentumWard` (CLI `--wake-ward`) and `balancedCellTransportKernel` (CLI `--balanced-cell`) added to [spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.mjs](../../../../scripts/braid-ideal/spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.mjs); central solver `AbsoluteHistoryRootRuntime.mjs` untouched; owner suite green (chunked). Seed/native-reconstruction grade; **no native force-free release authorized or run.** Fail-closed.

**The instrument.** `genericFieldClosures` generalizes the §82 `braidFieldClosures` to an arbitrary list of moving point sources, reconstructing $\mathbf E_{\mathrm{full}}$ (canonical $W^{\mathrm{rec}}=D_s/(D_s^2+\mathrm{soft}^2)$ at a stationary sphere), $\mathbf E_{\mathrm{static}}$ (drop the $D_s$ branch factor), and $\mathbf E_{\mathrm{anti}}$ through the same certified causal-root pipeline; when only the six rigid braid sites are passed it reduces to the §82 closures exactly. The wake angular-momentum current $J^i_{L_z,\mathrm{wake}}$ is measured as the far-field flux $\Phi_z(R)=\oint R^3\sin\theta\,\langle f_r f_\phi\rangle\,d\Omega$ through the same stress quadrature, with the **mandatory convergence guard**: a regulator soft-sweep plus a caustic-resolution grid refinement (double the angular/time grid). A non-convergent completion is reported as such, never massaged into a number (the §80 $A_{\mathrm{wake}}$-curl artifact is not repeated — the reconstruction is the canonical force, soft-extrapolated).

**Result 1 (Phase 1) — the bare-braid regression: the machinery converges and reproduces the §82 bound field.** With no sea, the braid-only wake $L_z$-flux is bound: endpoint slopes $\approx-1.9$ (a $1/r^2$ field, $\Phi\sim1/R$), the far-field flux is $\approx2\times10^{-3}$ of the pump, the soft-sweep slope range is $\approx0.006$, the caustic-resolution ratio is $\approx0.78$ ($<3$), and the root residual is $\sim10^{-14}$. The far-field wake carries $\approx0$ of the pump out, so the completed cycle-averaged identity leaves the $+0.424$ pump **reactively stored** (the Ward defect does not disappear via a flux): pinned/gapped, $\mathcal K_L(0,0)=0$. This is the clean control — the completion is constructible and convergent on a sub-luminal record.

**Result 2 (Phase 1) — the jh11 co-orbiting cage record: superluminal caustic, route a.** On jh11's `globalDrainDynamicalSea` half-co-orbiting cage the same-record mechanical decomposition reproduces the record exactly — $\texttt{seaOwnPump}=-0.6545$ at $\mathrm{frac}=\tfrac12$, $q_{AB,z}=\texttt{Lexport}+\texttt{braidReaction}\approx0$. But the cage equatorial endpoints orbit at $\Omega_{\mathrm{orb}}|\mathbf X_c|\approx1.21>c_f$ — **superluminal** — so the source-normal branch $D_s=c_f-\mathbf v\cdot\hat{\mathbf r}$ crosses zero and the wake reconstruction hits a genuine emission caustic on the record. The far-field falloff stays bound (slopes $\approx-1.9$ to $-2.05$, no $1/r$ radiation tail), but the flux **magnitude does not settle under the regulator**: the outer flux scales $\approx0.0011\to0.0145$ across $\mathrm{soft}\in\{0.08,\dots,0.01\}$ (ratio $\approx12.8$, still moving $\sim68\%$ per halving at $\mathrm{soft}=0.01$). The completion is **regulator-non-convergent at the $D_s=0$ caustic (route a)**: the $-0.65$ sits on an unresolvable emission caustic and cannot be certified as a transportable current. Reported as non-convergent, not massaged; the machinery itself converges (Result 1), so this is a property of jh11's specific superluminal record, corroborating its own doomed verdict.

**Result 3 (Phase 2) — the minimal balanced pro/anti cell is intrinsically pumped.** The memo's §8 model cell — braid$+$ (pump $+\hat{\mathbf z}$) at the origin, braid$-$ (spin-reversed, pump $-\hat{\mathbf z}$) at $\pm a_{\mathrm{cell}}\hat{\mathbf x}$, periodic images supplying bonds both directions — has intrinsic pumps $p_+=+0.424$, $p_-=-0.424$, coarse sum zero. But the coarse $p_++p_-=0$ is not sufficient; microscopic local balance is required. The directed cross torques are $T_{+\leftarrow-}\approx-0.0074$ and $T_{-\leftarrow+}\approx+0.0033$ (certified roots, residual $\sim10^{-15}$): the cross-hit relay absorbs only $\approx1.7\%$ of the pump (the §14 ejective $\approx1\%$ relay), and the reactive wake export is $\approx0$ (Result 1). So the local boundedness equations do **not** close: $p_++T_{+\leftarrow-}+W_+\approx+0.417$ and $-p_-+T_{-\leftarrow+}+W_-\approx-0.421$ — each $\approx$ the full un-cancelled pump. The cell is **intrinsically pumped**: outcome 3 of §8, not an admissible retained homogeneous sea state. Because the cell fails admissibility, the transport-kernel extraction is not licensed (the twist-response $\mathcal K_L^{(1)}$, $\mathcal K_L^{(2)}$ are computed as diagnostics but are moot on an inadmissible cell — a reactive lag is not transport).

**Verdict.** `BARRED (held to proof)`. Phase 1 constructs a convergent same-record wake completion on the sub-luminal control (the $+0.424$ pump is reactively stored, $\mathcal K_L(0,0)=0$), and the jh11 $-0.65$ is shown to sit on a superluminal $D_s=0$ caustic whose completion is regulator-non-convergent (route a) — so the $-0.65$ is neither a demonstrated relocation nor a certified transport current, exactly the memo's diagnosis. Phase 2's minimal balanced pro/anti cell is intrinsically pumped: the reactive cross-hit relay absorbs only $\approx2\%$ of the sign-definite pump, so no admissible balanced branch carries the drain, and the transport route is barred at the admissibility gate (before any $\mathcal K_L^{(2)}$ question). Combined with §85 (no self-consistent dynamical sea drains the pump) and §86 (the flutter is a genuine instability), this closes the same-record-wake sub-calculation the memo pinned as the blocker: a mechanical-plus-reactive-wake ledger cannot supply a secular $L_z$ drain of the bound braid's sign-definite pump. A theorem-level BARRED is **held to proof** (this is a seed/native-reconstruction-grade instrument on a coarse cell, not a continuation-certified theorem). Claim level: **seed-grade native reconstruction (canonical $W^{\mathrm{rec}}$, soft-extrapolated, caustic-resolution-checked) plus a coarse model-cell decider; not a native release; no release authorized.** Fail-closed: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`.

Reproduce: `node scripts/braid-ideal/spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.mjs --wake-ward --pretty` (bare control: add `--no-sea`; `--frac` sets the orbit fraction) and `node scripts/braid-ideal/spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.mjs --balanced-cell --pretty` (`--a-cell` sets the lattice spacing).

Next closure goal: hand the Phase-1 Ward-closure numbers ($\texttt{seaOwnPump}=-0.6545$, $q_{AB,z}\approx0$, bound-but-regulator-non-convergent wake flux at the superluminal caustic) and the Phase-2 intrinsically-pumped cell verdict to codex for an independent audit against the memo's decision rule; if codex accepts route a plus the admissibility bar as a theorem-level global BARRED, record the global no-go and return $A_0$'s $E_{\mathrm{internal}}$ to the far-field radiation-with-replenishment route (§76) as the sole surviving sink; if a sub-luminal balanced cell (orbit fraction below the $D_s=0$ threshold, or a non-orbiting axis-reversed lattice) is required to complete the decider, build that convergent cell and re-run the local-boundedness and twist-response gate before any localized-pump solve.

## 90. Nonlinear Saturation of the Axis-Sector Flutter: the Tracked $2.41246$ Whirl Is SUBCRITICAL, the Cubic Landau Term Reinforces Growth, and Direct Reduced-Axis Integration Finds No Bounded Jitter Cycle (2026-07-11)

The linear pencil establishes onset but cannot decide whether the growing whirl saturates. This section continues the same `gyroscopicTiltAnalysisFull` axis sector through cubic order using the runner's finite-rotation, constant-rate causal-history torque closure. The central solver is untouched. The two exact global-orientation directions are removed with the local slice $eta_O=0$, leaving four relative tilt coordinates and their four rates. This is a seed-grade reduced-axis model, not a native release.

**Exact linear regression and tracked onset.** At zero added damping the reduced model reproduces the source pencil bit for bit:

$$
\operatorname{Re}\lambda=0.19885688497216406,
\qquad
\operatorname{Im}\lambda=2.41245971901678.
$$

The continuation follows this high-frequency pair, rather than switching to the nearby low-frequency growing pair. It crosses the imaginary axis at added isotropic damping $d_H=0.1719136$, with $lambda_H\approx-3.7\times10^{-7}+2.4328197i$. The lower-frequency pair is still unstable there, with growth $+0.17096$ at frequency $0.31232$; therefore even a stable cycle on the tracked branch would not by itself close the full axis sector.

**Cubic normal form.** Let $A$ be the eight-state slice Jacobian at $d_H$, let $q,p$ be the normalized right and adjoint Hopf eigenvectors, and let $B,C$ be the quadratic and cubic derivative tensors of the measured reduced vector field. The standard Hopf contraction gives

$$
G_{21}
=
\left\langle p,
C(q,q,\bar q)
B\!\left(\bar q,(2i\omega_HI-A)^{-1}B(q,q)\right)
-2B\!\left(q,A^{-1}B(q,\bar q)\right)
\right\rangle,
\qquad
l_1=\frac{\operatorname{Re}G_{21}}{2\omega_H}.
$$

The measured values are $G_{21}=0.19963-0.59936i$ and

$$
l_1=+0.04103.
$$

With amplitude defined as the maximum relative layer tilt in the $eta_O=0$ slice, the real Landau cubic coefficient in $\dot a=\mu a+c_3a^3+O(a^5)$ is $c_3=+0.38572$ in inverse-time per radian squared. The sign is positive: the Hopf bifurcation is **subcritical**, so the cubic term reinforces the growing side and supplies no nearby stable small-amplitude limit cycle. The finite-difference step change $0.035\to0.030$ moves $l_1$ only from $+0.041029$ to $+0.041074$ and preserves the classification.

**Direct cubic integration.** An independent RK4 evolution of the full eight-state cubic Taylor system at the undamped base cell, seeded at $10^{-4}$ radians on the tracked mode, grows past the declared one-radian weak-tilt validity boundary near $t=51$. No amplitude plateau appears, `bounded=false`, and `saturatedAmplitudeRad=null`. This is not a claim of a literal infinite-amplitude trajectory outside the cubic chart; it is the fail-closed result that no bounded jitter exists within the weakly nonlinear neighborhood tested here.

**Verdict.** `subcritical_or_directly_divergent_seed_grade_flutter_is_fatal_at_cubic_order`. The Landau sign is positive and there is no saturated amplitude to report. The quiet-void no-go arc is therefore not reframed as an isolated-linear artifact: its fatal axis-sector reading survives the cubic extension. A native confirmation gate may reproduce or supersede this reduced result, but the present seed does not justify handing forward a benign zitterbewegung candidate. Fail-closed: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`.

Reproduce: `node scripts/braid-ideal/spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.mjs --nonlinear-flutter --pretty`. Owner test: `node --test --test-name-pattern="§90 nonlinear flutter" tests/braid-ideal-spindle-support-ratio-targeted-search.test.js`.

Next closure goal: run a native force-free retained-history confirmation of the subcritical axis-sector verdict and require the native amplitude envelope either to remain unbounded through the weak-tilt chart or to exhibit a bounded cycle that falsifies the reduced cubic result.

## 91. Kapitza Dynamic Stabilization of the Flutter: Does Buffeting Quiet It? — No; the Averaged Restoring Term Raises the Whirl Frequency but Never Quenches the Positive Growth Rate (2026-07-11)

Testing whether the §61/§63 quiet-void flutter is the wrong frame: rapid external buffeting can stabilize a negative-stiffness mode through the Kapitza mechanism, so the sea's continual agitation might be the missing stabilizer rather than an added threat. Runner + fixtures only: [kapitza-flutter-stabilization.mjs](../../../../scripts/braid-ideal/kapitza-flutter-stabilization.mjs) reads the already-measured coefficient matrices exposed by `gyroscopicTiltAnalysisFull`; the central solver remains untouched. The drive is a seed-grade stand-in for the sea, not a derived sea response. No native force-free release is authorized or run.

**Declared drive and averaging.** The measured deflated axis pencil is

$$
M\ddot q+C\dot q+S_0q=0,
\qquad
S_0=\Gamma-K,
$$

with the two exact global-tilt directions removed from the readout. A zero-mean additive force cannot change the eigenvalues of a linear pencil, so the only Kapitza-capable stand-in is a multiplicative axis-stiffness drive. In mass-normalized coordinates let $P_{\mathrm{rel}}$ project out the two global-tilt directions and define $H=M^{1/2}P_{\mathrm{rel}}M^{1/2}$. The coherent model is

$$
M\ddot q+C\dot q+\left[S_0+a\cos(\Omega t)H\right]q=0.
$$

Writing $q=Q+\xi$ and eliminating the fast response through order $\Omega^{-2}$ gives

$$
S_{\mathrm{eff}}=S_0+\frac{a^2}{2\Omega^2}H.
$$

For the broadband seeded stochastic model, $a\cos(\Omega t)$ is replaced by 64 random-phase spectral components across a one-octave band, with fixed seed `91091`; after normalization to total RMS amplitude $a_{\mathrm{rms}}$, the averaged correction is

$$
\Delta S_{\mathrm{stoch}}
=a_{\mathrm{rms}}^2\left(\sum_j\frac{w_j}{\Omega_j^2}\right)H,
\qquad \sum_jw_j=1.
$$

Thus both models test the same positive-semidefinite restoring direction but weight the fast spectrum differently. This is the method-of-averaging result; it does not assume that the sea actually couples through $H$.

**Regression.** With the drive off, the transformed runner reproduces the source pencil bit for bit: $\operatorname{Re}\lambda=0.19885688497216406$ and $\operatorname{Im}\lambda=2.41245971901678$. The fixture pins these values to $10^{-12}$ and independently reconstructs the spectrum from the exposed $M,C,S_0$ matrices.

**Threshold map — no crossing exists.** The coherent sweep uses $\Omega/\operatorname{Im}\lambda\in\{4,6,8,12,16\}$, safely above the whirl. For every frequency, increasing $a$ from zero through the declared search ceiling produces no $\operatorname{Re}\lambda\le0$ crossing: `thresholdPeakAmplitude=null`. Because the averaged spectrum depends only on $c_K=a^2/(2\Omega^2)$, the frequency rows collapse onto one coefficient map. A logarithmic sweep over $c_K\in[10^{-8},10^6]$ at $0.1$-decade spacing has its minimum growth at the unforced point itself, $\operatorname{Re}\lambda=+0.1988569$; the growth increases immediately with restoring strength and approaches about $+0.267$ while the whirl frequency rises without bound. The one-octave stochastic row, centered at $8\operatorname{Im}\lambda$ with effective frequency $17.98$, gives the same no-crossing verdict: `thresholdRmsAmplitude=null`. Therefore there is no amplitude-frequency quench curve to report inside the averaged model; the threshold set is empty.

**Why Kapitza fails here.** The bare relative-tilt block is already statically restoring. The instability is carried by the non-self-adjoint gyroscopic/circulatory velocity and torque structure, not by a negative stiffness that the Kapitza term can reverse. Adding $\Delta S\succeq0$ makes the mode oscillate faster but does not remove its positive energy-transfer rate. This is the multidimensional circulatory-flutter failure mode of the inverted-pendulum analogy: restoring stiffness alone does not supply damping or cancel the non-conservative circulation.

**Rough sea-scale comparison.** The already-declared local sea scales give $c_f/a\sim1/3.4\approx0.29$ for neighbor transit and at most the named fast-alignment scale $2\omega_{\mathrm{braid}}\approx2.08$. These are only $0.12$–$0.86$ of the flutter frequency, whereas the averaging map begins at $4\operatorname{Im}\lambda\approx9.65$. The known seed scales therefore do not enter the high-frequency regime, and no sea buffeting amplitude has been derived. This frequency mismatch is secondary, however: even granting arbitrarily large high-frequency multiplicative forcing in the favorable relative-tilt direction does not quench the averaged flutter.

**Verdict.** `negative_no_averaged_restoring_threshold_flutter_not_quenched`. Neither coherent periodic forcing nor broadband stochastic forcing dynamically stabilizes this axis mode under the seed-grade averaged Kapitza stand-in. A physically plausible buffeting threshold is not merely unsupported; no formal threshold exists in the declared favorable restoring family. The bobber intuition is not confirmed for this flutter, and the quiet-void no-go remains the correct seed-grade reading. Claim boundary: this closes the effective-averaged multiplicative-stiffness hypothesis only. A native sea-coupled record could still alter the circulatory or velocity block, but that would be a different mechanism, not Kapitza stabilization by fast restoring modulation. Fail-closed: `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`.

Reproduce: `node scripts/braid-ideal/kapitza-flutter-stabilization.mjs --pretty`; owner fixtures: `node --test tests/braid-ideal-kapitza-flutter-stabilization.test.js`.

Next closure goal: derive the native sea fluctuation spectrum and its full axis-sector coupling tensor on one self-consistent sea-coupled record, then test whether the measured forcing changes the circulatory/velocity block; do not revisit pure fast restoring modulation unless that native tensor contains a qualitatively different coupling direction.

## 92. Contra-Rotating Pro/Anti Pair Native Self-Sinking Instrument (2026-07-12)

Testing the one pairing that attacks the two measured single-braid mechanisms with their own signs. Runner + fixture only: [contra-rotating-pro-anti-pair-instrument.mjs](../../../../scripts/braid-ideal/contra-rotating-pro-anti-pair-instrument.mjs) consumes the existing exact-causal-root single-braid pump and completed six-coordinate axis pencil; [contra-rotating-pro-anti-pair-fixture.mjs](../../../../scripts/braid-ideal/contra-rotating-pro-anti-pair-fixture.mjs) declares the pair geometry and scan. The central solver remains untouched. No native retained-history release is authorized or run because the pair fails the linear gates before release.

**Declared ansatz.** The primary geometry is an axially stacked coaxial pair on the shared axis $\hat{\mathbf z}$, with axial separation $\Delta z=1$ in middle-radius units and relative phase $\Delta\phi=\pi$. The comparison geometry is coincident-coaxial, $\Delta z=0$, with the same relative phase. The pro copy has rotation sense $s=+1$; the anti copy has $s=-1$ and sitewise polarity conjugation. These are explicit pairing hypotheses, not derived bound geometries. The current linear instrument does not consume $\Delta z$ or $\Delta\phi$ because no same-record cross-braid retained-history force and torque rows exist yet; that omission is carried into the locking verdict rather than hidden.

**Single-braid regressions.** Removing either partner recovers the source runner exactly. In a common sign convention,

$$
\dot L_{z,+}=+0.4240300292341333,
\qquad
\dot L_{z,-}=-0.4240300292341333,
$$

and each isolated copy has

$$
\operatorname{Re}\lambda=0.19885688497216406,
\qquad
\lvert\operatorname{Im}\lambda\rvert=2.41245971901678.
$$

The anti pencil is the axis-reflected similarity conjugate of the pro pencil. This reverses the handed cross-axis circulatory blocks while preserving the isolated spectrum, so the second braid is a conjugate copy rather than a non-representable reversal of the three layers inside one braid.

**Pump result — algebraic cancellation only.** The intrinsic pair-boundary ledger sums to zero to machine precision. This is the intended sign attack, but it is not yet a genuine self-sinking result: the missing cross-braid interaction could add a secular torque or transfer the same defect into the relative coordinate. Accordingly, `crossPairContributionMeasured=false` and `genuinelySelfSinkingEstablished=false`. The pump gate passes only at the declared two-copy boundary.

**Joint flutter result — cancellation does not quiet the pair.** The free pair is the analytic union of the two isolated spectra, so its leading growth remains $+0.19885688497216406$. A finite relative-lock scan adds equal-and-opposite coupling to the two copies. For lock stiffness $k_{\mathrm{lock}}\in\{0.01,0.1,1,10,100\}$ with a small declared splitting regularizer $d_{\mathrm{lock}}=0.001$, the leading growth rates are respectively

$$
0.19925865,
\ 0.22035117,
\ 0.25017605,
\ 0.19853142,
\ 0.19641689.
$$

Every row grows. The hard-lock counterfactual is more decisive: its handed velocity-circulatory cross-block cancels exactly, yet the common pair mode still has

$$
\operatorname{Re}\lambda=+0.19629953398461314,
\qquad
\lvert\operatorname{Im}\lambda\rvert=0.38230688156911646.
$$

Thus the opposite handed terms do not drive the leading growth to marginal or stable. They reorganize the unstable spectrum into a slower whirl without removing its positive growth. This is not a cleanly stable neutral object.

**Locking and $\xi\to0$ continuation.** No finite native cross-pair locking mechanism has been derived, and every declared lock row remains linearly unstable. The pair therefore does not qualify as one coherent co-moving object at this grade. With no locked, linearly closed branch, the $\xi\to0$ luminal continuation is not reached and no near-lock residue can be assigned to the neutrino channel. Neither photon-channel nor neutrino-channel status follows from this seed.

**Verdict.** `pump_cancels_only_at_the_declared_boundary_but_flutter_survives_and_no_native_lock_exists_no_release`. The ansatz cancels one ledger entry but does not close the actual object: joint flutter survives even in the hard-lock limit, and native representability is absent. The problem is therefore relocated into the pair's common/relative axis dynamics rather than resolved. Fail-closed: `nativeRetainedHistoryReleaseAuthorized=false`, `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`.

Reproduce: `node scripts/braid-ideal/contra-rotating-pro-anti-pair-instrument.mjs --pretty`. Owner test: `node --test --test-name-pattern="§92" tests/braid-ideal-contra-rotating-pro-anti-pair-instrument.test.js`.

Next closure goal: derive the same-record cross-braid retained-history force, torque, phase-lock, and axial-separation rows for the declared coaxial ansatz, then rebuild the joint pencil from those measured cross blocks; authorize a native release only if that measured pair both locks and drives the complete quotient spectrum marginal or stable while preserving the zero net axial pump.

## 93. Contra-Rotating Pair Cross-Coupling Completion (2026-07-12)

Executing the §92 next closure goal. Runner + fixture only: [contra-rotating-pro-anti-cross-coupling.mjs](../../../../scripts/braid-ideal/contra-rotating-pro-anti-cross-coupling.mjs) measures both directed cross-braid force and torque rows through the production root API, and [contra-rotating-pro-anti-cross-coupling-fixture.mjs](../../../../scripts/braid-ideal/contra-rotating-pro-anti-cross-coupling-fixture.mjs) declares the separation/phase grid, derivative steps, and tolerances. The central solver remains untouched.

**Regression.** Removing the partner recovers the single-braid axial pump $+0.4240300292341333$ and the single-braid leading flutter growth $\operatorname{Re}\lambda=0.19885688497216406$. The §92 non-interacting pair anchors also reproduce exactly: free-pair growth $0.19885688497216406$ and imposed hard-lock growth $0.19629953398461314$.

**Measured geometry search.** The shared-record scan covers 96 cells: eight axial separations $\Delta z\in\{0.5,0.75,1,1.25,1.5,2,2.5,3\}$ and twelve relative phases over $[0,2\pi)$. Each static cell books all 72 directed cross-braid relations over four cycle samples. The selected refined cell has 288 retained roots, maximum root residual $9.97\times10^{-13}$, and minimum source-receiver distance $0.3288$, above the declared collision floor.

The coarse grid shows that the cross torque can pass through the magnitude required to cancel the intrinsic pump, but no coarse cell simultaneously closes the pump and axial rows. A two-variable Newton continuation then solves the individual pro-braid pump row and the relative axial-force row together. It converges in two corrections to

$$
\Delta z=1.419842173795055,
\qquad
\Delta\phi=3.8435815410366416.
$$

At that cell the cross torque transfers the intrinsic pump between the two braids rather than leaving it as ansatz bookkeeping:

$$
\dot L_{z,+}^{\mathrm{total}}=-1.27754\times10^{-4},
\qquad
\dot L_{z,-}^{\mathrm{total}}=+1.27754\times10^{-4}.
$$

The relative axial-force residual is $-5.42047\times10^{-4}$ and the common axial force is $-3.64\times10^{-16}$. Thus the cross-torque-completed pair genuinely closes the seed-grade axial pump and force ledgers at one non-colliding geometry. This is a pump/force equilibrium row, not yet a bound object.

**Native locking test — saddle, not lock.** Linearizing the relative phase torque and relative axial force about the equilibrium gives

$$
J_{\phi z}
=
\begin{pmatrix}
-2.93502462 & -2.18991851\\
-2.16016883 & 12.10340682
\end{pmatrix},
$$

with eigenvalues

$$
\mu_1=+12.41165549,
\qquad
\mu_2=-3.24327329.
$$

One direction restores and one direction expels. The measured equilibrium is a saddle, so the two braids do not lock into one coherent co-moving object. The pump can self-sink at the equilibrium point, but the geometry cannot hold that point without an imposed constraint.

**Measured joint axis pencil.** Static cross-torque derivatives and delayed tilt-rate derivatives produce full $12\times12$ cross blocks. Their largest absolute entries are $17.0299$ and $1.57850$, respectively. Inserting these measured blocks into the two-braid gyroscopic-circulatory pencil does not leave the original flutter unchanged and does not quiet it. The decomposition is

$$
\operatorname{Re}\lambda_{\mathrm{static\ cross}}=+5.43033398,
\qquad
\operatorname{Re}\lambda_{\mathrm{rate\ cross}}=+3.97325485,
$$

and the completed pencil has

$$
\operatorname{Re}\lambda_{\mathrm{joint}}=+5.30422826,
\qquad
\lvert\operatorname{Im}\lambda_{\mathrm{joint}}\rvert\approx0.
$$

The shift from the isolated-braid growth is $+5.10537138$. Cross coupling therefore touches the axis sector strongly, but in the wrong direction: it converts the original growing whirl into a much faster real divergence. Pairing neither cancels nor merely leaves the flutter alone; it adds a stronger cross-geometry instability. The separate non-rigid internal-axis route remains necessary if a stable pair channel is to exist.

**Verdict.** `measured_cross_pair_fails_native_lock_no_release`. The pair passes the cross-torque-completed pump gate but fails both the native lock gate and the complete quotient-spectrum gate. No native retained-history release is authorized. Claim level: seed-grade same-record cross-braid production-root measurement; `retainedBranchClaim=false`, `scoreMovement=no_score_increase`.

## 94. Flutter Radiative-Shedding Native Run: the All-Rail Barrel Loses Shape, the Tilt-Isolated Canonical Far Field Vanishes With Radius, and No Radiative Limit Cycle Exists (2026-07-12)

This is the native retained-history test of whether the §86/§90 flutter and an outgoing far-field angular-momentum current are one throughput. Runner [flutter-radiative-shedding-native-run.mjs](../../../../scripts/braid-ideal/flutter-radiative-shedding-native-run.mjs) reuses the production `runRelease` retained-history evolution and the §82 canonical antisymmetric reconstruction

$$
\mathbf E_{\mathrm{anti}}=\mathbf E_{\mathrm{full}}-\mathbf E_{\mathrm{static}}.
$$

The release function's shared `tiltPerturbation` surface rotates released positions and velocities while leaving the held prehistory unchanged. The central solver is untouched. Fail-closed; priority-only native diagnostic; no retained branch or score movement.

**Seed and control.** The height-preserving all-rail barrel is obtained from `SELF_EQUILIBRATED_V5` by setting every cylindrical radius to

$$
\rho_\ast=\cos\alpha_M=0.9600986791,
\qquad
\omega=\rho_\ast^{-1}=1.041559604,
$$

while retaining each layer's signed height and phase. Hence every layer satisfies $\omega R_L\cos\alpha_L=1$. The best-fit held-family coupling is $\kappa_\ast=0.03192843$. A release-instant relative tilt of $+0.1^\circ$ on I and $-0.1^\circ$ on M seeds the nutation sector; O is unchanged. An otherwise identical unperturbed barrel supplies the pump/radial control.

**Amplitude trajectory — no bounded cycle.** The tilt-seeded run starts at $0.0502^\circ$, reaches $7.57^\circ$ at $t=0.5$, $21.11^\circ$ at $t=1.0$, and a maximum $34.729^\circ$ near $t=3.0$. The later decrease is not saturation: the fitted spin collapses from $1.0415$ to $0.01554$, the maximum shape deviation grows to $11.07$, and the native run halts at

$$
t=7.5075\quad(1.195\ \text{initial rotations})
$$

on `shape_loss_radius_runaway` when M+ reaches radius $12.00019$. The tilt perturbation aggravates the failure: its maximum layer speed is about $1.606$, whereas the unperturbed control stays near $1.050$ but also loses shape at $t=11.495$. Neither trajectory is a bounded periodic object, and the apparent tilt rollover is the collapse of organized rotation during dispersal, not a limit cycle.

The time-step witness at $dt=0.00125$ preserves the qualitative verdict. The tilt reaches $33.75^\circ$, the fitted spin again collapses to $0.0121$, and M+ again triggers `shape_loss_radius_runaway`, now at $t=9.175$ ($1.460$ initial rotations). The halt clock shifts because the exact all-rail seed sits on the regulator-sensitive source-normal boundary, but halving $dt$ does not produce a returning orbit, a steady spin, or a bounded cycle.

**Tilt-sector isolation.** On each retarded source-time slice, write $\mathbf E=\mathbf E_0+\delta\mathbf E$, where $\mathbf E_0$ is the unperturbed barrel field. The angular-momentum flux vector is evaluated as

$$
\boldsymbol\Phi(R,t)=\oint_{S_R}\mathbf r\times\left[E_r\mathbf E\right]dA,
$$

and decomposed exactly into baseline, cross, and pure-tilt pieces. The isolated nutation contribution is $\boldsymbol\Phi_{\mathrm{nut}}=\boldsymbol\Phi_{\mathrm{total}}-\boldsymbol\Phi_0=\boldsymbol\Phi_{\mathrm{cross}}+\boldsymbol\Phi_{\mathrm{tilt}}$. This matched-control subtraction removes the common pump/radial channel without treating the quadratic cross term as zero.

**Far-field result — bound, not outgoing.** Retarded observation times $t_{\mathrm{obs}}=R+t_{\mathrm{source}}$ ensure that spheres $R\in\{12,16,24,32\}$ see the release rather than only its held prehistory. Across regulator values $\mathrm{soft}\in\{0.08,0.04,0.02\}$, the isolated transverse-flux slopes are

| source time | transverse slope range |
|---:|---:|
| $1.5015$ | $-2.47$ to $-2.15$ |
| $3.75375$ | $-0.864$ to $-0.836$ |
| $6.006$ | $-2.40$ to $-2.34$ |

Every row decays with radius; none approaches the radius-independent slope $0$ required for a nonzero $\Phi_\infty$. At the last source slice and tightest regulator, the isolated flux at $R=32$ is

$$
\boldsymbol\Phi_{\mathrm{nut}}(32)=(9.57\times10^{-7},\,-3.51\times10^{-7},\,5.87\times10^{-6}),
$$

with transverse magnitude $1.02\times10^{-6}$ and total norm $5.95\times10^{-6}$. Its norm falls with slope $-1.56$, so the measured asymptotic result is

$$
\boldsymbol\Phi_{\infty,\mathrm{nut}}=\mathbf0
$$

under the §82 canonical stress reconstruction. The outer-surface regulator ratios improve from $1.26$ to $1.13$ to $1.03$ across the three time slices. Certified root counts are stable under doubled scan density, maximum root residual is $1.42\times10^{-14}$, and no tangent roots or inactive-root gaps appear. Doubling the final outer-surface angular grid from $(N_\theta,N_\phi)=(6,12)$ to $(12,24)$ changes the isolated-flux norm by a factor $0.901$; the small vector's direction remains cancellation-sensitive, but its vanishing magnitude and non-radiative radial scaling survive.

**Decision.** `NO_NATIVE_BARREL_RELEASE_DIVERGES_OR_FAILS_TO_SETTLE_radiative_equilibrium_not_observed`.
